- **cross-validated-auc-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating BGC classification models on MIBiG-labeled Pfam TSV data and you need cross-validated AUC ROC values to compare machine-learned classifiers against an antiSMASH rule-based baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-auc-benchmarking))
- **cross-validated-auc-estimation**: Use when fitting a supervised multi-omics model (e.g. MB-PLS with latent variables) and you need a cross-validated AUC estimate plus tuned hyperparameters like the optimal number of latent variables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-auc-estimation))
- **cross-validated-auc-evaluation**: Use when you have labeled genomic region sets (e.g. HOT vs COLD) with per-region sequence features such as 2-4-mer frequencies, GC content, and CpG O/E ratio, and need an honest accuracy estimate via cross-validated AUC plus feature-importance rankings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-auc-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-auc-evaluation**: Use when you have a binary gene cluster absence/presence matrix with literature-assigned lifestyle labels and need to quantify how predictable each lifestyle is via cross-validated AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-auc-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-auroc-benchmarking**: Use when comparing supervised multi-omics classifiers (molecular-level vs pathway-level) on semi-synthetic or real labelled data and you need unbiased generalisation performance reported as AUROC across effect sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-auroc-benchmarking))
- **cross-validated-binary-classifier-performance-evaluation**: Use when a fitted penalized regression model on high-dimensional genomic covariates with a binary outcome (e.g., NMIBC vs MIBC invasion subtype) needs an unbiased estimate of prediction performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-binary-classifier-performance-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-classification-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when a trained LDA or HSS-LDA model with a priori labels (e.g., cell-cycle phase, day, cell type) must be validated by cross-validated classification accuracy and analysis of which classes are confused. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-classification-accuracy-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-classification-metrics-evaluation**: Use when 'relevant'/'other'-labeled HR-MS/MS spectra (e.g., SMARTS-filtered MSnLib v6 MGF files split by polarity and collision energy) have MS2DeepScore embeddings and you must train and evaluate classifiers with balanced-accuracy metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-classification-metrics-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-classifier-benchmarking**: Use when a small labeled training set (e.g., 364 sequences) must be split across multiple classifier and feature-selection methods to identify the -performing combination before independent testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/cross-validated-classifier-benchmarking))
- **cross-validated-classifier-performance-evaluation**: Use when a final, QC-passed LC-MS feature table with sample metadata (e.g., age groups like adolescence vs middle-age) must be turned into a phenotype classifier and its performance quantified via ROC AUC and 5-fold cross-validated accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-classifier-performance-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-classifier-training**: Use when labeled 'relevant' and 'other' HR-MS/MS reference spectra (e.g., MSnLib or in-house libraries) must be trained into binary classifiers whose cross-validated sensitivity and specificity per polarity/CE setup must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-classifier-training--727a582d5c33c0c5))
- **cross-validated-classifier-training**: Use when you have a manually labeled set of Pass/Fail peaks from an untargeted LC–MS metabolomics dataset and need to train, cross-validate, and statistically select among competing classifiers rather than trust a single model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-classifier-training--bea6ff2e5801f50d))
- **cross-validated-classifier-tuning**: Use when a longitudinal metabolomics dataset has a binary outcome label (e.g. recovered vs deceased COVID-19 patients) and you need a supervised classifier whose predictive performance is benchmarked via cross-validated AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-classifier-tuning))
- **cross-validated-concordance-index-estimation**: Use when evaluating the predictive performance of penalized Cox regression (Ridge, Lasso, adaptive Lasso) fitted to high-dimensional genomic covariates (e.g., 9,548 mRNA entries or 670 mutated genes vs. 316 patients' survival times) with a 5-fold cross-validated concordance index. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-concordance-index-estimation))
- **cross-validated-hyperparameter-grid-search**: Use when you must choose both a classifier (e.g., Random Forest vs XGBoost) and a feature-set variant (e.g., EI-only vs ESI-only vs combined binned mass spectra) and need fair, cross-validated hyperparameter tuning plus a single held-out metric for the decision. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-hyperparameter-grid-search))
- **cross-validated-hyperparameter-grid-search**: Use when fitting a regularized (L1/Lasso) mixture model — e.g., STENSL source selection with sink profile x vs. Y^T*α — and the sparsity level must be chosen from data rather than fixed arbitrarily. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validated-hyperparameter-grid-search))
- **cross-validated-hyperparameter-selection**: Reference for tuning hyperparameters of the semi-supervised rescoring classifier (e.g. ristretto/percolator SVM c) when rescoring peptide-spectrum matches with MS²Rescore and the train_fdr and model (svm vs lda) must be set alongside a tuned regularization parameter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-hyperparameter-selection))
- **cross-validated-hyperparameter-tuning**: Use when training retention-time prediction models on HILIC or RPLC libraries (e.g. Retip training sets with CDK descriptors) and you need tuned models plus an overfitting check comparing training vs test/validation errors. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-hyperparameter-tuning))
- **cross-validated-knn-imputation**: Use when a benchmark requires KNNimpute error rates on data with artificially introduced missing values (10%, 20% or 30% MCAR) but no a priori neighbour count k is given, so k_ must be chosen by cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-knn-imputation))
- **cross-validated-latent-variable-selection**: Use when fitting a PathIntegrate Multi-View MB-PLS (or comparable PLS-based multi-omics model) and the latent-variable count must be tuned to avoid over- or under-fitting before pathway importance is interpreted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-latent-variable-selection))
- **cross-validated-model-evaluation**: Use when you have paired microbial-feature and metabolite-count matrices for the same samples and need an honest estimate of how well a predictive model recovers each metabolite across held-out folds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-model-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-model-performance-evaluation-10fold**: Use when when you have trained a neural network or regression model to predict metabolite abundances from microbiome features and need to measure how well the model generalizes to unseen samples. Apply this skill when you want to compare multiple competing models (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-model-performance-evaluation-10fold))
- **cross-validated-model-training-and-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a de novo sequencing model's generalization across species — i.e., annotated MS/MS spectra (MGF) must be split so each organism is held out in turn as a test fold while training on the remaining species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-model-training-and-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-neural-network-training**: Use when paired microbiome and metabolome count tables are available and the goal is to predict the community metabolome from microbial features with cross-validated SCC evaluation and background-shuffled significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-neural-network-training))
- **cross-validated-oplsda-score-scoring**: Use when you have cross-validated supervised model (e.g. OPLS-DA) scores for predefined pairwise class contrasts (carriage vs controls, serovar vs serovar) and need ROC curves, AUC values, and bootstrap CIs to report diagnostic performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-oplsda-score-scoring))
- **cross-validated-phenotype-trait-prediction**: Use when predicting phenotypic traits (e.g. aerobe, anaerobe, gramneg, thermophile) from comparative-genomics feature matrices (COG/eggNOG presence-absence profiles) and need cross-validated, kernel-comparable SVM accuracy and runtime figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validated-phenotype-trait-prediction))
- **cross-validated-prediction-evaluation**: Use when a multivariate logistic or Cox model has been built from genes selected by first-step screening (e.g., adaptive Lasso with FDR) and you need an unbiased estimate of prediction power via cross validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-prediction-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-predictive-ability-assessment**: Use when evaluating a supervised discriminant model (OPLS-DA) fitted to a metabolomics feature table with far more features than samples, to decide whether reported class separation reflects real predictive signal or overfitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-predictive-ability-assessment))
- **cross-validated-predictive-parameter-ranking**: Use when a peak table contains many candidate shape/quality features (e.g. duration, height, area, FWHM, tailing factor, asymmetry derivatives) and you must rank which features predict true vs false peaks before fitting a classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-predictive-parameter-ranking))
- **cross-validated-project-audit**: 对高风险项目、变更、PR、指定作者提交、安全问题或修复结果做风险驱动的多代理交叉审计：先固定范围与决策问题，再按 Risk → verification method → executor 派发异质只读调查；证据由工具代跑（run/mutate）、裁决由主代理独占（decide）、高严重度结论强制过一次盲化对抗复核（challenge），输出可追溯的报告与放行结论。用于用户明确要求交叉验证、发布/合并门禁、严格修复验证，或经确认的高风险多路安全/作者审计；不用于普通代码评审、快速摘要、纯风格检查和无需多路径验证的窄问答。 ([Leexunhuan743/cross-validated-project-audit](https://github.com/Leexunhuan743/cross-validated-project-audit/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **cross-validated-regularization-parameter-selection**: Use when fitting a graphical lasso to a high-dimensional feature table (e.g. 90 metabolites quantified by NMR) and you need an unbiased, data-driven choice of the L1 penalty alpha instead of a fixed guess. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-regularization-parameter-selection))
- **cross-validated-regularization-tuning**: Use when fitting suffPCR (row-sparse PCA + OLS/logistic) on high-dimensional, correlated omics data like the DLBCL expression set and the penalty lambda / subspace dimension d must be chosen by 5-fold cross-validation before extracting sparse loadings. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-regularization-tuning--a9ff45deb93136dc))
- **cross-validated-regularization-tuning**: Use when training a penalized multinomial classifier (e.g. glmnet) on per-cell signature-score features and the regularization strength must be tuned by k-fold cross-validation rather than fixed a priori. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-regularization-tuning--548dc571b63b9a90))
- **cross-validated-rmsd-evaluation**: Decision support for selecting or validating a chemical shift predictor (protein FM, RNA FM, or legacy SVR) when the target atom types are 1H, 13C, 15N and BMRB-assigned shifts plus matching PDB structures are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-rmsd-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-roc-auc-evaluation**: Use when a deep learning predictor (e.g., ncDeepBrain variant score) has been trained on labeled positives/negatives and you must quantify classification performance via cross-validated ROC curves and AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cross-validated-roc-auc-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-scc-evaluation**: Use when a paired microbiome-metabolome model must be evaluated per metabolite under repeated cross-validation, or when well-predicted metabolites must be counted against an empirical background threshold. - **cross-validated-search**: OpenClaw skill for source-backed web search, page reading, and evidence-aware claim checking. - **cross-validated-spearman-correlation-evaluation**: Use when comparing metabolite-prediction models (e.g., MiMeNet vs. MelonnPan or NED) on paired microbiome-metabolome datasets and you need per-metabolite SCC plus a count of well-predicted metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validated-spearman-correlation-evaluation))
- **cross-validated-threshold-estimation**: Use when predicting labels for an unlabeled scRNA-seq dataset against a trained hierarchical classifier and you need a data-driven cutoff to reject cells that belong to no known population. - **cross-validation-auroc-computation**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking CypReact's per-enzyme reactant prediction performance: you have the CypReact package, the 1632-molecule reactant dataset, and need cross-validation AUROC per CYP450 enzyme. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-auroc-computation))
- **cross-validation-benchmark-design**: Use when a claimed annotation-propagation algorithm must be evaluated for true generalization to metabolites lacking seeds — i.e., when you need a held-out benchmark of coverage and Top 1/3/10 correct rates rather than a single-shot annotation run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-benchmark-design))
- **cross-validation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when you have developed a predictive model and need to compare its performance against established baselines (e.g., linear regression, Random Forest, Canonical Correlation Analysis) across multiple datasets with paired input-output features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-benchmark-evaluation) · 2 copies)
- **cross-validation-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when quantifying top-1 and overall retrieval rates of de novo structure predictions on MS2 spectra (e.g. GNPS, GNPS-OK), or when ablating the modified Platt re-ranking step to isolate the RNN's raw ranking power. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-benchmark-evaluation))
- **cross-validation-benchmark-evaluation-scrnaseq**: Use when benchmarking cell-type classifiers on scRNA-seq data with predefined hierarchical trees, requiring k-fold cross-validated HF1-scores, rejection rates of unseen populations, and comparison to published baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-benchmark-evaluation-scrnaseq))
- **cross-validation-benchmark-of-cell-classifiers**: Use when benchmarking automated single-cell cell-type classifiers (e.g., SIMS vs scANVI, Seurat, Scnym, SingleR, SciBet) on pre-annotated datasets to quantify accuracy and Macro F1 under cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-benchmark-of-cell-classifiers))
- **cross-validation-benchmark-of-network-methods**: Use when comparing multiple network inference tools (e.g., pyPARAGON vs PL, OI2, DOMINO) on identical seed gene sets and a shared reference interactome, with known drivers as the validation ground truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-benchmark-of-network-methods))
- **cross-validation-classifier-evaluation**: Use when you must reproduce or estimate generalization performance of a per-isoform CYP450 bond-level predictor on EBoMD-style labeled [compound, bond] data, and need externally cross-validated Jaccard scores rather than a single hold-out split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-classifier-evaluation))
- **cross-validation-design**: モデルを何に使うかと、データの依存の構造 (同じ単位の繰り返し、空間の近さ、地域、時間) から分け方を選び、分割表を 1 つ作る。基準もモデルもその分割表で採点する。特徴量ができた後、基準を出す前に使う。 ([yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills](https://github.com/yuiseki/GeoAI-master-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/cross-validation-design) · ★3)
- **cross-validation-engine**: Run k-fold cross-validation (stratified, grouped, or time-series) when evaluating model generalization. Triggers on "k-fold CV", "StratifiedKFold", "GroupKFold", "TimeSeriesSplit", "nested CV", "mean plus minus std", "subject-level CV". ([HossamBalaha/SkillStack](https://github.com/HossamBalaha/SkillStack/tree/HEAD/ml-engineering/cross-validation-engine))
- **cross-validation-evaluation-data-processing**: Use when MS/MS spectra plus structure labels (e.g., GNPS cross-validation data) must be scored against database structure candidates to produce a top-k correct-identification curve for a structure identification tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-evaluation-data-processing))
- **cross-validation-evaluation-measure-analysis**: Use when multiple peak-quality metric sets (e.g., M4, M7, M11) must be paired with diverse ML algorithms and statistically compared via cross-validation to identify a -performing classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-evaluation-measure-analysis))
- **cross-validation-execution**: Execute cross-validation checks across M1/M2/M3 outputs to detect file list inconsistencies, risk scoring gaps, and completeness issues ([isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service](https://github.com/isdlc/isdlc-knowledge-service/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/impact-analysis/cross-validation))
- **cross-validation-experiment-design**: Use when fine-tuning a pre-trained retention-time model to a new chromatographic dataset and you need a stable estimate of R2 across folds and seeds rather than a single train/test split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-experiment-design))
- **cross-validation-fold-count-comparison**: Use when evaluating whether combining models from k-fold cross-validation splits raises protein contact prediction accuracy over a single model, and when comparing accuracy across fold counts (e.g., 3/5/7/10) on CASP-style benchmark sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-fold-count-comparison))
- **cross-validation-fold-model-consensus**: Use when classifying unlabelled genes as TSGs or OGs from a gene-by-feature mutation matrix (e.g. 37 ratio-metric/entropy features) where a single model would risk overfitting the small CGC-labelled training set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-fold-model-consensus))
- **cross-validation-fold-model-fine-tuning**: Use when reproducing the DeepMet training protocol: you have a pre-trained SMILES language model, a known-metabolite structure set (HMDB), and need per-fold fine-tuned generators whose withheld 10% mimics unknown metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-fold-model-fine-tuning))
- **cross-validation-fold-normalization**: Use when training per-feature-set ML models (e.g., random forest regressors on CCLE omics) where scaling must be fit only on training folds of a 5-fold CV scheme to avoid leakage into held-out metabolite predictions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-fold-normalization))
- **cross-validation-grid-search-hyperparameter-tuning**: Use when choosing among several candidate classifiers on a high-dimensional expression matrix (e.g. 19,737-gene TCGA RNA-seq with subtype labels) and you need unbiased per-model hyperparameter selection plus held-out generalization estimates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-grid-search-hyperparameter-tuning))
- **cross-validation-hyperparameter-stability**: Use when training a classifier on a small labelled gene set (e.g. 136 TSGs and 76 OGs from CGC) where overfitting risk is high and hyper-parameters must be estimated stably before predictions are trusted. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-hyperparameter-stability))
- **cross-validation-k-selection**: Use when inferring population structure from genome-wide biallelic SNP data (VCF- or PLINK-derived BED files) and the number of ancestral populations (K) is unknown and must be estimated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-k-selection))
- **cross-validation-metabolite-predictability-assessment**: Use when matched multiomic (CCLE-style) feature tables and a metabolite abundance matrix are available and the goal is to benchmark how well each omics class individually predicts each metabolite via cross-validated ML. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-metabolite-predictability-assessment))
- **cross-validation-model-aggregation**: Use when after training multiple neural network models via k-fold cross-validation (e.g., 10 iterations × 10-fold = 100 models), when you need to extract and aggregate learned feature interactions rather than relying on a single trained model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-model-aggregation))
- **cross-validation-model-assessment**: Use when a trained classifier (e.g., LGBM on BiLSTM/UniRep/TAPE-BERT feature subsets) must be validated before reporting performance, requiring both cross-validation and an independent test evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-model-assessment))
- **cross-validation-model-evaluation**: Use when comparing versions of an in silico MS/MS spectral prediction model on a shared experimental dataset and you need fold-matched, per-energy-level metrics (Dice, Precision, Recall, Dot Product) with significance testing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cross-validation-model-evaluation))
- **cross-validation-model-evaluation**: Use when reproducing or verifying the Character Predictor of MicroPIE: trained SVM models over labelled taxonomic-description sentences must be evaluated with k-fold cross validation and compared to reported precision/recall. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-model-evaluation--d5ddcc78e0b0f47e))
- **cross-validation-model-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking supervised protein family classifiers built on amino acid recoding kmer vectors, and you need cross-validated ROC AUC/PR AUC to compare alphabets and kmer lengths. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-model-evaluation--726b04bfd2f2e85e))
- **cross-validation-model-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a cancer driver gene prediction model on directed gene regulatory networks (DawnNet, KEGG, RegNetwork) with multi-omics gene features and you need 5-fold CV metrics (AUPRC, AUROC, accuracy) comparable to graph-based baselines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-model-evaluation))
- **cross-validation-neural-network-training**: Use when you need to train or robustness-test EmptyNN's PU-learning classifier over a grid of threshold T (50-400), cross-validation folds k (5-20), and iterations N (5-20) on raw droplet scRNA-seq count matrices. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-neural-network-training))
- **cross-validation-of-commission-schedules-over-time**: Use when a multi-year US equity backtest applies today's zero commissions to trades from 2016; models the schedule that actually applied on each date. Volume-tiered rates belong to exchange-fee-tier-and-rebate-structure-analysis. ([HimanshuJ16/Algo-Trading-Skills](https://github.com/HimanshuJ16/Algo-Trading-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cross-validation-of-commission-schedules-over-time))
- **cross-validation-of-metagenomic-models**: Use when a trained metagenomic classification model (e.g. random forest on DIABIMMUNE T1D profiles) needs robustness verification beyond a single train/test split, or when overfitting/bias must be ruled out before reporting accuracies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-of-metagenomic-models))
- **cross-validation-of-probabilistic-models**: Use when a probabilistic distribution model (e.g. the cubic-spline fragment length model in Forseti) must be trained on empirical measurements and its accuracy/variation on held-out data must be quantified with RMSE and k-fold cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-of-probabilistic-models))
- **cross-validation-parameter-selection**: Use when fitting FateCompass's linear model of gene regulation on single-cell data and needing to control model complexity via the diffusion step t, which must be tuned by cross-validation rather than set heuristically. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-parameter-selection))
- **cross-validation-parameter-tuning**: Use when when building Cox-PH or Cox-nnet survival models from expression or metabolomic feature matrices paired with event/time vectors, and you need to select optimal regularization strength (alpha), cross-validation fold count (nfold), risk stratification method, and optimization strategy before. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-parameter-tuning))
- **cross-validation-partitioned-feature-selection**: Use when you need to identify which sequence-position features (e.g., position-specific gapped k-mer descriptors on 41-nt RNA sequences) are consistently, not just incidentally, predictive across repeated cross-validation resampling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-partitioned-feature-selection))
- **cross-validation-pcc-evaluation**: Use when you need to score a ΔΨ regression model on the CAGI-5 vex-seq data by 10-fold cross-validation on TR957 plus a blind test on TS1098, comparing predicted vs. measured delta-PSI via PCC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cross-validation-pcc-evaluation))
- **cross-validation-performance-estimation**: Use when a fingerprint-based binary classifier (e.g. the metabolite-likeness SVM over compounds from HMDB, ChEBI, LipidMaps, YMDB vs ZINC non-metabolites) needs an unbiased accuracy estimate before it is applied to score compound databases. - **cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking a DNA 6mA (or similar sequence-classification) predictor against an existing tool under matched 5-fold cross-validation on benchmark datasets such as cross-species, rice, or M. musculus. - **cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when comparing two or more BGC detectors on the same labeled Pfam training matrix and you need a fair, split-identical performance estimate such as mean ROC AUC across folds. - **cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when when you have paired microbiome and metabolome count data and need to estimate how well a neural network or regression model can predict metabolite abundances from microbial features without overfitting to a single held-out test set. - **cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when comparing GPCR identification methods (e.g., EMCBOW-GPCR vs. Liao's SVM-Prot 188D + SMOTE + RF) under a stated SMOTE-balancing protocol, so that per-fold Acc/AUC can be aggregated into a fair side-by-side metrics table. - **cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when validating an m5C site prediction model on a balanced training set (e.g., 5,298 pos/5,298 neg A. thaliana or 4,563 pos/4,563 neg M. musculus 41-bp sequences) and reporting 10-fold cross-validation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-performance-evaluation--be16b2bef980997c))
- **cross-validation-performance-evaluation**: Use when reproducing or benchmarking a small-sample gene-classification model (e.g. 136 TSGs and 76 OGs labelled from CGC) and you need stable, class-wise performance metrics under k-fold cross-validation rather than a single train/test split. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cross-validation-performance-evaluation--303f408dfd49b859))
- **cross-validation-performance-metric-computation**: Use when a curated gene set or biomarker panel must be validated computationally by measuring normal-vs-cancer classification performance (Accuracy, AUC, F1) under cross-validation against competing gene signatures. - **cross-validation-performance-reproduction**: Use when reproducing RT-Transformer-based candidate filtering: given MS-Finder candidate compounds with measured RTs from MoNa/Metabobase and a transfer-learned model, verify counts of correct/erroneous annotations and ranking shifts. - **cross-validation-performance-table-parsing**: Use when a paper reports aggregate performance counts over a phenotype/class table and you must recompute those counts from a supplementary per-class accuracy table to verify the claim.
